Judges three axes: Screaming Architecture (does the name reveal the domain?), Clean Code (full words, intention-revealing, no abbreviations), and Ubiquitous Language (vocabulary consistency across the codebase). Useful to validate that names self-document. ([DGouron/shiplens](https://github.com/DGouron/shiplens/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/naming-audit) · ★1)
- **naming-audit**: Audit and fix names so each file, module, folder, type, service, unit, route and CLI verb says what it does from its name alone - no metaphors (door.py), no vague nouns (utils, model.py), no near-twins - in any language. Load it when arpad asks 'what is X.py?', 'rename', 'these names are bad', 'naming audit', or questions a name; when a plan introduces new files, modules or commands (check them before the plan is shown); and before a build is called done. Lists every failing name with alternatives and a pick, then renames everything and its references in one pass. ([arpadav/aav-brain](https://github.com/arpadav/aav-brain/tree/HEAD/skills/naming-audit))
